"<div id=""rubric"">4000 Essential English Words</div>
<div style='font-family: Arial; font-size: 70px;color:#FF80DD;'>agree</div>
<hr>
[sound:01_0001.mp3]<hr>
<div style='font-family: Arial; font-size: 70px;color:#FF80DD;'>əˈɡriː</div>
"	"<div style='font-family: Arial; color:#FF80DD;'>agree</div>
<hr>
<img src=""01_0001.jpg"">
<hr>
<div  style='font-family: Arial; color:#00aaaa; text-align:left;'>
Meaning: To <i>agree</i> is to have the same opinion or belief as another person.</div>
<hr>
<div  style='font-family: Arial; color:#9CFFFA; text-align:left;'>
&nbsp;→&nbsp;Example: The students <b>agree</b> they have too much homework.</div>

<div  style='font-family: Arial; color:#9CFFFA; text-align:left;'>
&nbsp;→&nbsp;Example: We removed the outer case to reveal the computer’s <b>internal</b> wires.</div>

<hr>
<div  style='font-family: Arial; color:#00aaaa; text-align:left;'>
Meaning: A <i>poll</i> is a survey in which people give their opinions about important things.</div>
<hr>
<div  style='font-family: Arial; color:#9CFFFA; text-align:left;'>
&nbsp;→&nbsp;Example: The <b>poll</b> showed that many people support the plan to stop gang violence.</div>
